+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    return count * elementSize;
+}
+
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
     }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    return count * elementSize;
+}
+
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
     }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+void generateOptions(HostOptions& options) {

+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    return count * elementSize;
+}
+
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
     }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};
+

+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    return count * elementSize;
+}
+
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
     }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }
 
-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}
+

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }
 
-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}
+

+struct HostOptions {

+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {

+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }

+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }
 
-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}
+

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.

+struct HostOptions {

+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {

+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }

+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;

+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;

+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }

+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}

    94	// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
    95	// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
    96	// one operation.
    97	struct HostOptions {
    98	    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
    99	        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
   100	
   101	    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
   102	        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
   103	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
   104	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   105	        }
   106	        return count * kDoubleFields;
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
   110	    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
   111	    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
   112	    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
   113	    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
   114	    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
   115	
   116	    size_t count;
   117	    std::vector<double> doubles;
   118	    std::vector<int> types;
   119	};
   120	
   121	// Standard test cases for validation.
   122	inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
   123	    return {{
   124	        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
   125	        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
   126	        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
   127	        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
   128	        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
   129	        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},

   134	// Generate the same contracts as the original implementation, directly into
   135	// the layout consumed by the CUDA kernel.
   136	void generateOptions(HostOptions& options) {
   137	    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
   138	
   139	    if (options.count == 0) {
   140	        return;
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    double* const strike = options.strike();
   144	    double* const spot = options.spot();
   145	    double* const q = options.q();
   146	    double* const r = options.r();
   147	    double* const t = options.t();
   148	    double* const vol = options.vol();
   149	
   150	    for (size_t i = 0; i < options.count; ++i) {
   151	        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
   152	        const double factor =
   153	            1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
   154	
   155	        options.types[i] = base.type;
   156	        strike[i] = base.strike * factor;
   157	        spot[i] = base.spot * factor;
   158	        q[i] = base.q;
   159	        r[i] = base.r;
   160	        t[i] = base.t;
   161	        vol[i] = base.vol;
   162	    }
   163	}
   164	
   165	__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
   166	    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
   167	}
   168	
   169	__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
   170	    const int* __restrict__ types,
   171	    const double* __restrict__ strike,
   172	    const double* __restrict__ spot,
   173	    const double* __restrict__ q,
   174	    const double* __restrict__ r,
   175	    const double* __restrict__ t,
   176	    const double* __restrict__ vol,
   177	    double* __restrict__ results,
   178	    const size_t count) {
   179	    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   180	    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
   181	
   182	    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
   183	        const double time = t[i];
   184	        const double sigma = vol[i];
   185	
   186	        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
   187	            results[i] = 0.0;
   188	            continue;
   189	        }
   190	
   191	        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
   192	        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
   193	        const double rate = r[i];
   194	        const double dividendYield = q[i];
   195	        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
   196	        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
   197	        const double d1 =
   198	            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
   199	             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
   200	            sigmaSqrtTime;
   201	        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
   202	        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
   203	        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
   204	
   205	        if (types[i] == CALL) {
   206	            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
   207	                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
   208	        } else {
   209	            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
   210	                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
   211	        }
   212	    }
   213	}
   214	
   215	[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
   216	    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
   217	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
   218	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   219	    }
   220	    return count * elementSize;
   221	}

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }
 
-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}
+

+    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
     bool allPassed = true;

+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+        spot[i] = base.spot * factor;

+        vol[i] = base.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }
 
-bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
-                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
+// Transfers are deliberately outside the measured interval. The reported time
+// is the GPU pricing computation, matching the original benchmark's compute-only
+// timing boundary.
+double priceOptionsCuda(const HostOptions& options, double* const hostResults) {
+    int device = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
+
+    if (options.count == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t doubleBytes = checkedBytes(options.doubles.size(), sizeof(double));
+    const size_t typeBytes = checkedBytes(options.types.size(), sizeof(int));
+    const size_t resultBytes = checkedBytes(options.count, sizeof(double));
+
+    CudaBuffer deviceDoubles;
+    CudaBuffer deviceTypes;
+    CudaBuffer deviceResults;
+    deviceDoubles.allocate(doubleBytes);
+    deviceTypes.allocate(typeBytes);
+    deviceResults.allocate(resultBytes);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDoubles.get(), options.doubles.data(), doubleBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceTypes.get(), options.types.data(), typeBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    int minimumGridSize = 0;
+    int threadsPerBlock = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxPotentialBlockSize(
+        &minimumGridSize, &threadsPerBlock, blackScholesKernel, 0, 0));
+
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device));
+
+    const size_t requiredBlocks =
+        options.count / static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) +
+        (options.count % static_cast<size_t>(threadsPerBlock) != 0);
+    // A bounded grid avoids excessive block-scheduling overhead for very large
+    // inputs; the grid-stride loop retains arbitrary-size scalability.
+    const size_t residentWaves = std::max<size_t>(
+        static_cast<size_t>(minimumGridSize),
+        static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32);
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(requiredBlocks, residentWaves));
+
+    const auto* const deviceInput = static_cast<const double*>(deviceDoubles.get());
+    const auto* const deviceType = static_cast<const int*>(deviceTypes.get());
+    auto* const deviceOutput = static_cast<double*>(deviceResults.get());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceType,
+        deviceInput,
+        deviceInput + options.count,
+        deviceInput + 2 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 3 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 4 * options.count,
+        deviceInput + 5 * options.count,
+        deviceOutput,
+        options.count);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start.get(), stop.get()));
+
+    if (hostResults != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostResults, deviceOutput, resultBytes,
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
+}
+

+    cudaEvent_t event_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+// Structure-of-arrays storage gives every warp coalesced loads for every field.
+// The six double arrays share one allocation so they can also be transferred in
+// one operation.
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const size_t size)
+        : count(size), doubles(checkedDoubleCount(size)), types(size) {}
+
+    static size_t checkedDoubleCount(const size_t count) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / kDoubleFields) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        return count * kDoubleFields;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] double* strike() noexcept { return doubles.data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* spot() noexcept { return doubles.data() + count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* q() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 2 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* r() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 3 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* t() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 4 * count; }
+    [[nodiscard]] double* vol() noexcept { return doubles.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    size_t count;
+    std::vector<double> doubles;
+    std::vector<int> types;
+};

+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__global__ void blackScholesKernel(
+    const int* __restrict__ types,
+    const double* __restrict__ strike,
+    const double* __restrict__ spot,
+    const double* __restrict__ q,
+    const double* __restrict__ r,
+    const double* __restrict__ t,
+    const double* __restrict__ vol,
+    double* __restrict__ results,
+    const size_t count) {
+    const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = first; i < count; i += stride) {
+        const double time = t[i];
+        const double sigma = vol[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+            results[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double stockPrice = spot[i];
+        const double strikePrice = strike[i];
+        const double rate = r[i];
+        const double dividendYield = q[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(stockPrice / strikePrice) +
+             (rate - dividendYield + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+            sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+        const double discount = exp(-rate * time);
+        const double discountedStock = stockPrice * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+
+        if (types[i] == CALL) {
+            results[i] = discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+                         strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+        } else {
+            results[i] = strikePrice * discount * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d2) -
+                         discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(-d1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] size_t checkedBytes(const size_t count, const size_t elementSize) {
+    if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / elementSize) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested option count is too large\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    return count * elementSize;
 }
